This release 9 added, 3258 changed API endpoint(s).

:::danger Breaking change
This release removes endpoints or adds newly-required request fields. Review the **Removed** and **Changed** sections before upgrading your integration.
:::

<!-- truncate -->

## Added

- `GET /api/purchase-orders` — List Purchase Orders (DataTable)
- `GET /api/return-reasons` — List Return Reasons
- `GET /api/sales-credits` — List Sales Credits
- `GET /api/supplier-inventories` — List Supplier Inventories (DataTable)
- `GET /api/supplier-pricing-tiers` — List Supplier Pricing Tiers
- `GET /api/suppliers` — List Suppliers
- `POST /api/pos/transactions/{posTransaction}/receipt/send` — Send POS Receipt (E-Receipt)
- `POST /api/quotes/{quote}/convert` — Convert Quote
- `PUT /api/purchase-orders/{purchase_order}/update-shipment` — Update Shipment Details (FBA)
